OrthoPilot autonomously retrieves real-time imaging, laboratory, pathology and order data and converts evolving patient states into evidence-based decisions from admission diagnosis to rehabilitation planning. We established a specialist-validated benchmark from real-world electronic health records spanning 1,000 disease codes. In a reader study across the complete care pathway, OrthoPilot was compared with 81 orthopaedic physicians and surpassed experts with 25 years of experience in diagnostic reasoning, clinical decision-making and management planning. It also outperformed all evaluated intelligent systems across 60 external clinical centres. In a prospective study of 1,870 complex cases, OrthoPilot increased full-chain management success by 10.6%. During an 8-month randomised deployment involving 8,240 inpatients, it increased cumulative cases per bed by 9.7% and improved patient-reported access to health information. These results move clinical AI from predicting isolated events toward executing longitudinal management across complete musculoskeletal care pathways.

</details>


### [77] [Vertical Standardisation for High-Risk AI Systems under the EU AI Act: A Domain-Specific Framework for Algorithmic Hiring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12588)
*Anna Gatzioura,Vrettos Moulos,Nina Baranowska*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对欧盟AI法案对高风险AI系统的要求，提出了面向算法招聘（尤其是基于排名的招聘系统）的标准化建议，涵盖风险管理、数据治理、可解释性、人类监督和部署后监控等领域，提供了一个垂直领域特定的标准化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟AI法案要求高风险AI系统符合特定标准，但算法招聘领域尚缺乏专门的欧洲标准，现有方法多为横向通用框架，无法充分覆盖该领域的独特挑战。

Method: 将AI法案要求映射到具体标准化建议，聚焦招聘系统的生命周期歧视风险、公平感知数据治理、可解释性、人类监督和部署后监控，提出垂直领域特定框架。

Result: 提出了面向算法招聘，特别是基于排名的招聘系统的领域特定标准化建议框架，该框架不依赖特定技术实现，可适配替代方法、工具或治理机制。

Conclusion: 现有横向AI治理方法不足，需要垂直领域的标准化框架来应对算法招聘中的独特挑战；本文建议源于FINDHR项目但具有普适性。

Abstract: According to the recent European legislation, high-risk AI systems will have to adapt in order to comply with requirements related to specific areas, like risk management, data quality and governance, logging and traceability, technical documentation, transparency, human oversight, and accuracy, as outlined in the European Artificial Intelligence (AI) Act. As the standardisation process for AI is expected to remain iterative and, so far, there are no European standards on AI fully covering the challenges of algorithmic hiring, we propose specific standardisation-oriented recommendations related to the relevant AI areas specified by the European Commission. For each of these areas, we set the context by describing the requirements that AI systems in high-risk domains, and especially in recruitment, should fulfil, as well as the activities that should be carried out to ensure their appropriate use and desired performance, in line with the requirements deriving from the AI Act. Unlike existing horizontal approaches to AI governance and standardisation, this paper contributes a vertical, domain-specific framework for algorithmic hiring, and especially ranking-based recruitment systems, by mapping the requirements of the AI Act to concrete standardisation recommendations, focusing on lifecycle discrimination risks, fairness-aware data governance, explainability, human oversight, and post-deployment monitoring in recruitment systems. Even though our recommendations were informed by the outcomes of the European project FINDHR, they are not tied to the project's technical artefacts and could be implemented using alternative methods, tools, or governance mechanisms.

</details>


### [78] [Agentic Service-Oriented Computing: A Manifesto for the Next Frontier of Service-Oriented Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12619)
*Amin Beheshti,Rong N. Chang,Boualem Benatallah,Fabio Casati,Schahram Dustdar,Geoffrey Fox,Quan Z. Sheng,Yan Wang,Jian Yang,Albert Zomaya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Service-Oriented Computing (ASOC)作为新的研究与工程领域，将LLM驱动的自主/半自主智能体视为服务进行工程化，通过服务计算领域二十余年积累的工程原则（组合、互操作、治理、信任等）来规范化当前ad hoc的智能体AI生态，并提出了六项基础原则和五维研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的自主/半自主智能体正从孤立的认知原型转向复杂分布式工作流，面临组合、互操作、服务质量、生命周期管理、治理、安全与信任等挑战——这些正是服务计算社区已研究二十余年的问题。然而当前智能体AI生态系统缺乏工程严谨性，不足以支撑可靠的企业及社会级部署。

Method: 通过系统化提炼服务计算领域的核心原则，将其适配到智能体AI场景，提出ASOC的六项基本原（可驾驭性、可组合性、生命周期工程、可信赖性设计、目标驱动编排、可观察性与问责性），并构建涵盖五个维度的研究议程。

Result: 定义了ASOC作为新领域：将智能体工程化为服务、通过自主/半自主智能体编排服务、在信任/网络安全/合规/性能/问责约束下治理智能体与服务生态系统；提出了六项原则和五维研究议程。

Conclusion: 服务计算社区能够为智能体AI提供概念与工程骨架，将智能体AI从碎片化演示转变为值得人类与组织信赖的、基于服务的可靠系统。

Abstract: The rapid emergence of LLM-powered autonomous and semi-autonomous agents is reshaping software systems from static, request-response components into goal-directed, adaptive, and tool-using computational actors. As these agents move from isolated cognitive prototypes into complex distributed workflows, they confront challenges that the Service-Oriented Computing community has studied for more than two decades: composition, interoperability, quality of service, lifecycle management, governance, security, and trust. Yet much of today's agentic AI ecosystem is developing these foundations ad hoc, without the engineering rigour required for dependable enterprise and societal deployment. This paper introduces Agentic Service-Oriented Computing (ASOC) as a new research and practice area concerned with engineering agents as services, orchestrating services through autonomous and semi-autonomous agents, and governing ecosystems of agents and services under constraints of trust, cybersecurity, compliance, performance, and accountability. We articulate six foundational principles of ASOC (harness-ability, composability, lifecycle engineering, trustworthiness by design, goal-driven orchestration, and observability/accountability) and organise a five-dimensional research agenda spanning: (i) agentic services foundations and lifecycle engineering; (ii) composition, orchestration, and interoperability; (iii) governance, observability, and accountability; (iv) security, trust, and risk management; and (v) evaluation, certification, and Agentic QoS. We argue that the Services Computing community is especially well positioned to provide the conceptual and engineering spine for this emerging field, transforming agentic AI from fragmented demonstrations into dependable, service-based systems worthy of human and organisational trust.

</details>


### [79] [Atomic Units of X: The Compression Layer of Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12634)
*Sachin Dev Duggal,Pradyumna Swarnalatha Ramanna,Alexandros Vassiliades*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出智能的本质是原子压缩与组合复用的理论框架，通过压缩演算（Compression Calculus）形式化表面表示与原子表示的差异，并论证每一层抽象带来的表征效率呈乘法级增长（Compounding Cascade thesis），指出当前AI系统处于次优表示层级，应将大语言模型视为动态融合引擎而非完整知识架构，为自演化知识系统设计提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能系统（包括认知系统、生物系统、计算系统）缺乏统一的理论框架来解释智能的可扩展性，尤其是如何通过分解复杂现象为可复用的原子单元并重组为高阶结构来实现效率、迁移性、可解释性和可进化性。

Method: 提出压缩演算（Compression Calculus）作为形式化框架，用于比较表面级表示与原子表示，描述压缩增益在抽象层间的累积方式；引入复合级联论题（Compounding Cascade thesis），量化抽象层的乘法级效率提升。

Result: 当前AI系统普遍处于次优表示层级（如token级处理或文档级检索），缺乏稳定的概念级原子结构；大语言模型更适合被理解为动态融合引擎而非完整知识架构。

Conclusion: 该框架为设计能够随时间发现、精炼和组合新原语的自演化知识系统提供了基础，通过将智能重新定义为通过组合抽象实现的压缩过程，统一了专业知识、知识表示、可解释AI和自适应智能系统的未来架构。

Abstract: This paper proposes a theoretical framework for understanding intelligence as a process of atomic compression and compositional reuse. We argue that cognitive, biological, computational, and organizational systems achieve scalable intelligence by decomposing complex phenomena into reusable atomic units that can be recombined into higher-order structures. Drawing on evidence from cognitive science, information theory, evolutionary biology, software engineering, medicine, legal reasoning, education, music, and artificial intelligence, the paper develops the concept of atomic units as fundamental compression layers that support efficiency, transfer, interpretability, and evolvability. The central contribution is the Compression Calculus, a formal framework for comparing surface-level representations with atomic representations and for describing how compression gains compound across abstraction layers. We introduce the Compounding Cascade thesis, according to which each additional layer of abstraction multiplicatively increases representational efficiency rather than merely adding incremental savings. The paper further argues that contemporary AI systems often operate at suboptimal levels of representation, relying on token-level processing or document-level retrieval rather than stable, concept-level atomic structures. In this view, large language models are best understood not as complete knowledge architectures, but as dynamic fusion engines capable of navigating, sequencing, and recombining atomic units. The framework provides a foundation for designing self-evolving knowledge systems that can discover, refine, and compose new primitives over time. By reframing intelligence as compression through compositional abstraction, the paper offers a unifying perspective on expertise, knowledge representation, explainable AI, and the future architecture of adaptive intelligent systems.

</details>


### [80] [A Learning-Rate-Gated Failure of GRPO in a Small Language and Vision-Language Model Web Agent: A Controlled Null and Its Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12640)
*Chengguang Gan,Zhixi Cai,Yunhao Liang,Hanjun Wei,Shiwen Ni,Qinghao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在4B-8B规模的小型语言-视觉语言Web Agent上，GRPO（基于可验证奖励的强化学习）是否真正提升了技能。通过18组控制实验，发现GRPO在强监督基线已掌握的任务上无法可靠提升成功率，仅在存在提升空间（采样策略已优于贪心策略）时有效。失败原因在于中等学习率会降级模型、高学习率会崩溃模型，且两者存在双重分离现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究GRPO这类基于可验证奖励的强化学习方法在小规模（4B-8B）Web Agent上是否真正增加了模型技能，还是仅仅重塑了监督模型已有的行为模式，以检验强化学习在小模型上的实际效用边界。

Method: 设计了18组控制实验网格，变化学习率、KL权重、种子、初始化和裁剪等超参数，在文本和Set-of-Marks截图两种观察模式下，对4B和8B规模的Web Agent进行GRPO训练。通过配对检验、多训练种子和多评估种子进行统计验证，并设计对比实验（可采样提升的任务 vs 已掌握任务）来区分因果。

Result: 1) 无任何配置能在强监督基线上可靠提升已掌握任务的成功率，中等至高学习率甚至显著降低性能；2) 在存在提升空间的任务上，GRPO能提升22个百分点的成功率；3) 失败机制存在双重分离：中等学习率降级可定位到注意力与MLP模块，高学习率崩溃无法追溯到单一模块；4) 4B模型的有效秩与能力耦合，8B模型则解耦。

Conclusion: GRPO在小规模Web Agent上并未增加技能，仅在有提升空间时有效。其失效源于两种不同机制的学习率相关损害，且该现象存在尺度依赖性（4B vs 8B表现不同）。这为强化学习在小模型上的应用提供了重要边界条件。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) in particular, is now run routinely on a supervised checkpoint in the hope of producing a stronger agent. We ask whether it adds skill to a small language and vision-language model web agent at the 4B to 8B scale, or whether it mostly reshapes behavior the supervised model already has. Across a control grid of 18 runs that varies learning rate, KL weight, seed, initialization, and clipping, no configuration credibly improves the success rate of a strong supervised baseline on tasks the agent has largely mastered. On the text track, moderate to high learning rates make it credibly worse. The null holds under paired testing, 25 evaluation seeds, 6 training seeds, changes to the recipe, both text and Set-of-Marks screenshot observations, and scaling the backbone to 8B; the credible harm is a text-track finding and is only nominal under Set-of-Marks. To show that the null reflects the setting and not a broken pipeline, we run the identical harness, reward, and recipe on tasks whose reward is reachable by sampling, and there the success rate rises by 22 points with a paired interval that excludes zero. GRPO therefore helps only when there is headroom to climb, meaning the sampled policy already succeeds more often than the greedy one. We then explain the failure. A middle learning rate degrades the agent and a high one collapses it, and the two regimes form a double dissociation: grafting localizes the degrade regime to the attention and MLP blocks, while the collapse regime cannot be traced to any single group, and the embedding change that dominates the weight movement is causally inert. At 4B, effective rank in the late layers tracks capability in both directions; at 8B the two come apart. This coupling is specific to the smaller model, so we report it as scale-dependent.

</details>


### [81] [Internet of Agentic Things: Networked AI Agents for Closed-Loop IoT Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12662)
*Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了物联网体化（IoAT）架构，将智能体AI、物联网、信息物理系统、物理AI、边缘计算和数字孪生统一到一个闭环编排框架中，并形式化为耦合的工作流-控制问题，采用全态动态规划框架连接智能体规划与物理执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物联网和智能体AI系统缺乏统一的架构框架来整合感知、推理、协调和执行能力，无法实现跨分布式信息物理环境的高效闭环编排。

Method: 提出IoAT三层架构（云端、边缘/雾层、物理IoT层），通过自主AI智能体连接；形式化为耦合工作流-控制问题，采用全态动态规划框架处理嵌套的战略与战术决策。

Result: 以智能建筑编排作为代表性用例展示了框架的可行性，并识别了安全、保障、治理、韧性和可信部署等关键研究挑战。

Conclusion: IoAT为未来自主信息物理系统的统一编排提供了理论框架和实现方向，但仍需解决多个关键挑战才能实现可信部署。

Abstract: The paper introduces the Internet of Agentic Things (IoAT), an architectural framework that integrates agentic AI, IoT, cyber-physical systems, Physical AI, edge computing, and digital twins into a unified closed-loop orchestration framework. The proposed architecture consists of cloud, edge/fog, and physical IoT layers connected through autonomous AI agents that perceive, reason, coordinate, and actuate across distributed cyber-physical environments. The paper formalizes IoAT as a coupled workflow-control problem with nested strategic and tactical decision making using a hylomorphic dynamic programming framework that links agentic planning with physical execution. Smart-building orchestration is presented as a representative use case, and key research challenges related to safety, security, governance, resilience, and trustworthy deployment are discussed.

</details>


### [82] [MaxSAT-Based Feedback for Guiding Vision-Language Models in Sudoku](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12711)
*Pedro Orvalho,Guillem Alenyà,Felip Manyà*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号方法，通过MaxSAT求解器作为一致性验证和优化引擎，将形式化约束推理融入VLM的视觉推理过程，用于解决数独等网格谜题。VLM生成的候选解被编码为软子句，数独约束为硬子句，MaxSAT找出最大一致子集并生成结构化反馈指导VLM逐步优化，实验表明该方法显著提升了逻辑一致性和解题成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VLM在结构化视觉推理任务中展示了强大的感知能力，但缺乏显式的逻辑一致性强制机制，经常产生违反底层约束的赋值结果。

Method: 将VLM生成的候选放置编码为部分MaxSAT公式中的软子句，数独约束保持为硬子句；MaxSAT求解器找出最大一致子集后，将其转化为结构化的文本和视觉反馈，引导VLM进行后续优化。

Result: 在多个开源和闭源VLM上的实验表明，基于MaxSAT的反馈提升了逻辑一致性，增加了成功解题的数量，特别是在全盘优化模式下效果显著。

Conclusion: 符号优化能够增强视觉-语言推理的可靠性，神经符号方法可有效弥补纯神经方法在逻辑一致性上的不足。

Abstract: Vision--Language Models (VLMs) have recently demonstrated promising performance on structured visual reasoning tasks, including grid-based puzzles. However, despite strong perceptual capabilities, these models lack explicit mechanisms for enforcing logical consistency and frequently generate assignments that violate underlying constraints. In this paper, we propose a neuro-symbolic approach that integrates formal constraint reasoning into the VLM solving process via a Maximum Satisfiability (MaxSAT) oracle. Rather than computing solutions directly, the symbolic component acts as a consistency validator and refinement engine. Candidate placements generated by the VLM are encoded as soft clauses in a partial MaxSAT formulation, while Sudoku constraints remain hard clauses. When inconsistencies arise, the MaxSAT solver identifies a largest mutually consistent subset of assignments, which is then translated into structured textual and visual feedback to guide subsequent refinements. We evaluate our approach on a Sudoku dataset across multiple open-source and closed-access VLMs. Results show that MaxSAT-based feedback improves logical consistency and increases the number of solved instances, particularly in full-board refinement mode. These findings demonstrate that symbolic optimisation can enhance the reliability of vision-language reasoning.

</details>


### [83] [LLMs Can See the Smoke but not the Fire: Evaluating Abductive Reasoning with Elenchos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12733)
*Julius Steiglechner,Lucas Mahler,Gabriele Lohmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Elenchos框架，将溯因推理建模为结构逆问题，评估LLM在形式系统中检测和归因规则突变的能力。实验发现模型存在检测-归因分离现象，在交互突变场景下表现显著下降，且增加推理时间收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在模式识别和文本生成方面表现出色，但其进行溯因推理——从观察到的行为推断潜在假设——的能力尚不明确。现有评估方法缺乏对溯因推理的结构化度量，因此需要一种系统化的评估框架。

Method: 提出Elenchos框架，将溯因推理形式化为结构逆问题：给定参考形式系统（如λ演算）及其可能发生突变的变体，模型需判断是否发生突变，并推断导致行为差异的具体规则修改。对前沿和中端LLM进行系统评估。

Result: 模型呈现检测-归因分离：能识别系统被改动，但难以定位潜在突变；交互突变场景下性能显著下降，模型仅能恢复部分突变；增加推理时间带来的收益有限，仅获得适度改进。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在溯因推理方面存在根本性局限，尤其在归因环节和复杂交互场景下。检测与归因能力的分离暗示模型推理机制与人类溯因推理存在本质差异，为提升LLM推理能力提供了明确方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel at pattern recognition and text generation, but their capacity for abductive inference - inferring latent hypotheses that explain observed behavior - remains poorly understood. Here, we introduce Elenchos (named after the Socratic method of cross-examination), a generative evaluation framework that measures abductive reasoning as a structural inverse problem. Given a reference formal system, such as the lambda-calculus, and a potentially mutated counterpart, agents must determine whether a mutation has occurred and infer the rule modifications responsible for the resulting behavioral differences. Evaluating frontier and mid-tier LLMs reveals a consistent detection-attribution dissociation: models often recognize that a system has been altered but struggle to identify the latent mutations causing the observed discrepancies. Performance degrades substantially under interacting mutations, where models frequently recover only a subset of the underlying mutations. Preliminary evidence also suggests diminishing returns from increased inference-time reasoning, with only modest improvements under larger reasoning budgets, though this finding requires further validation.

</details>


### [84] [Tracing Agentic Failure from the Flow of Success](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12747)
*Samuel Yeh,Yiwen Zhu,Shaleen Deep,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OAT方法，将无监督失败归因建模为基于神经受控微分方程的单类学习问题，仅使用成功轨迹训练即可在推理时识别失败步骤，相比提示基线速度快200-5000倍且F1分数提升20%（域内）和7%（域外）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体系统的失败归因方法要么依赖提示管道（计算昂贵），要么需要带步骤级错误标注的失败轨迹进行后训练（标注成本高、难以扩展），亟需一种轻量级、无需失败数据步骤级监督的实用归因模型。

Method: 提出OAT（One-class learning with neural controlled differential equations for Attribution in agentic Trajectories），将失败归因转化为单类学习问题：利用神经受控微分方程在潜空间建模成功轨迹的动态模式，推理时对失败轨迹的每一步计算偏离该动态模式的异常分数，从而识别错误步骤。

Result: 仅用100条成功轨迹训练，OAT比提示基线快200-5000倍；域内数据集F1提升20%，域外数据集F1提升7%，且持续优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: OAT证明了基于成功轨迹的无监督学习是一种高效、可扩展的诊断智能体系统失败的前景方向。

Abstract: Failure attribution for LLM-based agentic systems, i.e., identifying which steps in a failure trajectory caused the task to fail, is critical for debugging and improving these systems. Existing approaches either rely on prompting-based pipelines, which are computationally expensive, or require post-training on failure trajectories with step-level error annotations, which are costly to collect and difficult to scale. We argue that a practical failure attribution model should be lightweight and trainable without step-level supervision on failure data. To this end, we address unsupervised failure attribution, i.e., training exclusively on successful trajectories and identifying error steps at inference time given a failure trajectory. We propose OAT, which casts this problem as one-class learning with neural controlled differential equations, modeling the dynamical pattern of successful trajectories in latent space. At inference time, each step in a failure trajectory is assigned an anomaly score based on its deviation from the dynamics learned on successful trajectories, which is then used to form a set of error steps. With training on only 100 successful trajectories, experiments show that OAT is 200--5000 $\times$ faster than prompting-based baselines, and, at the same time, consistently outperforms them in both in-domain and out-of-distribution datasets with +20% and +7% F1 scores, respectively, demonstrating that OAT is a promising and efficient direction for diagnosing agentic system failures.

</details>


### [85] [Accuracy and Normalized Accuracy under Length Bias: Analysis, Guidelines, and a Bayesian Alternative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12767)
*Koen Oostermeijer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文揭示了多项选择基准测试中基于条件对数概率的评分存在长度偏差：标准方法惩罚长答案，长度归一化则过度修正为偏好长答案。作者提出贝叶斯准确率，通过引入答案长度的显式先验计算后验概率，从而消除线性长度效应，该方法无需额外计算且在不同基准和少样本设置下均表现出更低的长短偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项选择基准测试中，基于条件对数概率的评分存在长度偏差：长答案因token求和而被惩罚，而常见的长度归一化补偿又过度修正，引入偏向长答案的偏差。作者旨在分析这两种评分规则的偏差特性，并提出一种更公正的替代方案。

Method: 首先分析了标准准确率和长度归一化准确率的长度偏差特性及其与答案长度分布的关系；基于此分析，提出了贝叶斯准确率（Bayesian accuracy），通过引入答案长度的显式先验分布，计算每个候选答案的后验概率，从而消除线性长度效应。该方法无需额外前向传播，可直接替换原有的似然评分。

Result: 实验表明，贝叶斯准确率在多个基准测试和少样本设置下，其经验长度偏差一致低于标准准确率和长度归一化准确率。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯准确率通过先验控制长度效应，是一种更公正、可直接替换的多项选择评估方法，有效缓解了长度偏差问题。

Abstract: Multiple-choice benchmarks that rank candidate completions by conditional log-probability suffer from a length bias: because log-probabilities sum over tokens, longer answers tend to be penalized relative to shorter ones in practice. A common mitigation is to normalize scores by completion length, but we show empirically that this heuristic frequently over-corrects, introducing a bias toward longer answers instead. We first analyze these scoring rules, characterizing when standard and length-normalized accuracy are appropriate and how their length biases depend on the distribution of completion lengths. Motivated by this analysis, we introduce \emph{Bayesian accuracy}, a scoring rule that computes the posterior probability of each candidate under an explicit prior over answer length, thereby removing linear length effects. Bayesian accuracy is a drop-in replacement for likelihood-based multiple-choice evaluation, requires no additional forward passes, and consistently exhibits lower empirical length bias than both standard and length-normalized accuracy across benchmarks and few-shot settings.

</details>


### [86] [Do We Really Need Multimodal Emotion Language Models Larger Than 1B Parameters?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12787)
*Kaiwen Zheng,Junchen Fu,Wenhao Deng,Hu Han,Joemon M. Jose,Xuri Ge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Light-MER框架，通过知识蒸馏将大规模多模态大语言模型（>7B）的知识迁移到轻量级学生模型（<1B），在保持多模态情感识别高性能的同时显著提升推理效率。引入最优传输损失（切片Wasserstein距离+隐藏状态对齐）和多奖励GRPO优化策略，在九个基准数据集上达到SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在多模态情感识别（MER）中性能提升依赖庞大的模型参数量（至少7B），导致计算成本高、推理效率低，难以在机器人、移动设备等资源受限平台上实时部署。作者质疑是否真的需要超过1B参数的模型来实现高质量MER，旨在探索轻量级模型的有效性。

Method: 提出Light-MER框架，核心为知识蒸馏：以大规模教师模型指导轻量级子十亿参数学生模型。创新优化策略包括：(1) 最优传输损失——结合切片Wasserstein距离与隐藏状态对齐；(2) 基于GRPO的多奖励优化策略，平衡MER性能与效率。

Result: 在九个基准数据集上，Light-MER达到SOTA性能，同时显著提升推理效率，证明轻量级模型在多模态情感识别中的巨大潜力。

Conclusion: 挑战了"更大模型必然更好"的假设，表明通过有效的知识蒸馏，子十亿参数的小模型可以兼顾高性能与高效率，为资源受限平台上的实时多模态情感识别提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have significantly improved the performance of multimodal emotion recognition (MER) and enabled interpretable description generation by jointly modeling video, audio, and language, etc. However, these performance improvements are often accompanied by an increase in model parameter size (e.g, at least 7B), which simultaneously incurs high computational costs and reduces inference efficiency, thereby hindering real-time deployment on resource-constrained platforms such as robots and mobile devices. This raises a fundamental question: do we really need the multimodal MER model larger than 1B parameters for high-quality MER?
  In this paper, we challenge the assumption that larger models are inherently necessary and proposes a lightweight MER framework (called Light-MER), which achieves better and faster multimodal sentiment understanding and recognition through knowledge distillation. It can transfer knowledge from a strong, large-scale teacher model to a lightweight sub-billion-parameter student model, aiming to preserve rich multimodal emotion reasoning and recognition while substantially improving deployment efficiency. Specifically, we introduce two new optimization strategies to enhance knowledge transfer: (1) a new optimal transport loss that combines Sliced Wasserstein Distance with hidden-state alignment, and (2) a new multi-reward optimization strategy based on GRPO that balances MER performance and efficiency, aimed at further enhancing the learning capabilities of student models. Extensive experiments on nine benchmark datasets demonstrate that Light-MER achieves state-of-the-art performance while significantly improving inference efficiency. This highlights the strong potential of small multimodal emotion language models for future research. Code is available at https://github.com/GAIR-Lab/Light-MER.

</details>


### [87] [Who Grades the Grader? Co-Evolving Evaluation Metrics and Skills for Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12790)
*Xing Zhang,Guanghui Wang,Yanwei Cui,Ziyuan Li,Wei Qiu,Bing Zhu,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种在缺乏可靠评估指标时，通过元进化循环自主演化评估指标，并与技能循环协同进化（Double Ratchet）的框架，在代码生成、文本转SQL等任务中保留了88-110%的理想指标提升效果，并通过锚定集审计和外部监督确保安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化智能体系统依赖一个隐藏假设：已存在可靠的评估指标。但在许多实际应用中这一假设不成立，因此需要一种无需预设指标即可实现自进化的方法。

Method: 提出"指标进化"方法：通过组合小型的缺陷检测器，在完整进化生命周期下搜索，与10个锚定参考集对齐训练，并在未标注输出上通过共识正则化，通过保留锚定集进行审计。进一步提出"Double Ratchet"框架，实现指标与技能循环的协同进化。

Result: 在代码生成（MBPP+）、企业文本转SQL（Spider 2.0-Snow）和免参考报告生成三个任务上，保留了由真实指标驱动时88-110%的提升效果；锚定守卫的移除导致指标坍塌为空洞检测器；当演化技能试图取巧时，独立裁判能有效检测并修复。

Conclusion: 该"预期失败"架构是在缺乏可靠自动验证器时的默认方案，通过锚定纪律和外部审计在自进化系统中实现了可解释、可审计的评估指标。

Abstract: Self-evolving agent systems improve by creating, revising, and retiring their own skills, but every such loop rests on a hidden assumption: a reliable evaluation metric already exists. In many real applications it does not. We make three claims. First, metrics can be \emph{evolved}: our metric loop searches compositions of small drawback detectors under a full evolutionary lifecycle, trained to agree with a ten-item anchored reference set, regularized by consensus over unlabeled outputs, and audited against a held-out anchor it never reads, yielding a transparent, inspectable metric rather than an opaque judge. Second, since no metric exists to beat, the yardstick is recovering what an accurate metric would have enabled, and \emph{Double Ratchet}, our co-evolution of the metric with a lifecycle-managed skill loop, does so: across code generation (MBPP+), enterprise text-to-SQL (Spider~2.0-Snow), and reference-free report generation, it retains 88--110\% of the held-out lift achieved by the same skill loop driven by ground truth or the best available rubric. Third, safety comes from anchor discipline plus outer audits: removing anchor guards collapses the metric into a vacuous detector while removing the lifecycle does not; and when evolved skills gamed the report rubric, an independent judge caught it, one detector repaired it, and a task-aware judge then preferred the evolved outputs over the pre-evolution baseline in 77\% of decided pairs. We argue this failure-expecting architecture is the right default wherever no reliable automatic verifier exists.

</details>


### [88] [Visual Access Boundaries in Vision-Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12815)
*Hiroto Osaka,Shohei Taniguchi,Gouki Minegishi,Kai Yamashita,Masahiro Suzuki,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过因果干预实验（Visual Access Sweep）发现，Chain-of-Thought（CoT）提示在视觉语言模型（VLM）中并非通过延长对图像令牌的直接访问来提升性能，而是主要通过扩展语言侧对图像衍生隐藏状态的计算。CoT的有效性受限于模型的感知读出能力（perceptual readout），而非计数能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管CoT被广泛用作VLM的测试时扩展策略，但其性能提升的机制尚不明确——CoT生成长推理轨迹时，究竟是在扩展对图像令牌的持续访问，还是主要通过已提取的视觉信息进行语言侧计算？

Method: 提出Visual Access Sweep因果干预方法，在生成过程中沿层深度和生成时间步遮蔽从生成令牌查询到图像令牌键的注意力，定义Visual Access Boundary（VAB）为保持任务精度的最小访问区域。在Qwen2.5-VL和InternVL3的6种模型配置上进行实验，并结合符号属性oracle和单目标探测-解码检查进行分析。

Result: (1) 无论是否使用CoT，VLM都表现出有限的VAB；(2) CoT的VAB层与no-CoT基线最多相差2层，尽管生成长度显著增加；(3) CoT仅在模型能够可靠读出视觉属性时有效，否则无效；(4) 符号属性oracle显示CoT能提升计数，但前提是提供真实属性文本；(5) 硬属性可从隐藏状态线性恢复，但模型自身难以输出，瓶颈在读出而非计数。

Conclusion: CoT并非通过延长图像令牌的持续访问来提升性能，而是通过扩展语言侧对已提取视觉信息的计算。CoT的增益受限于感知读出能力，瓶颈在于从隐藏状态到输出的映射，而非视觉信息的获取或计数本身。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting is widely used as a test-time scaling strategy for Vision-Language Models (VLMs), but it remains unclear what is extended when VLMs generate longer reasoning traces. We ask whether CoT requires continued access to image tokens, or whether it mainly operates over visual information already made available earlier in the forward pass. We introduce Visual Access Sweep, a causal intervention that masks attention from generated-token queries to image-token keys along layer depth and generation time, and define the Visual Access Boundary (VAB) as the minimal access region that preserves task accuracy. Across six model configurations from Qwen2.5-VL and InternVL3, both no-CoT direct answering and CoT prompting exhibit finite VABs. In Qwen2.5-VL-32B and InternVL3 at 14B and 38B scales, when CoT is evaluated against the no-CoT full-access target, its VAB layer differs from the no-CoT boundary by at most two layers, despite substantially longer generations. This suggests that CoT does not primarily improve performance by prolonging direct image-token access throughout the reasoning trace, but by extending language-side computation over image-derived hidden-state information. We further show that CoT gains are constrained by perceptual readout. CoT helps when the queried visual attribute can be reliably read out by the model, but not when that readout is unreliable. A symbolic-attribute oracle shows that CoT can improve counting once ground-truth attributes are supplied as text, while a single-object probe-vs-decode check shows that hard attributes can be linearly recoverable from hidden states yet difficult for the model itself to output. Together, these analyses place the bottleneck at readout rather than counting.

</details>


### [89] [Human-AI Agent Interaction as a Neuroplastic Training Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12823)
*Eranga Bandara,Ross Gore,Asanga Gunaratna,Ravi Mukkamala,Nihal Siriwardanagea,Gihan Siriwardanagea,Sachini Rajapakse,Isurunima Kularathna,Pramoda Karunarathna,Chalani Rajapakse,Sachin Shetty,Christopher K. Rhea,Ng Wee Keong,Kasun De Zoysa,Amin Hass,Shaifali Kaushik,Wathsala Herath,Preston Samuel,Anita H. Clayton,Atmaram Yarlagadd*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出人机交互中的迭代循环（请求-结果-评估-修正）实际上是一种高频的神经可塑性训练环境。当交互结果令人失望时，会反复触发不耐烦、完美主义、挫败感等反应模式，通过长时程增强作用强化这些神经通路。作者提出通过“幕后观察”——在认知前的感觉间隙中观察神经过程而非做出反应性重新提示——可以利用长时程抑制来弱化这些反应模式，从而将同一环境转化为有益的训练场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示日常AI交互中未被识别的神经可塑性训练效应——用户在与AI代理的迭代交互中，失望结果反复触发的反应模式会通过长时程增强被无意识地强化，从而可能加剧负面心理模式。

Method: 提出三层观察框架和两种应用模式：用户引导模式（不改变现有工具）和代理辅助模式（轻量配置AI代理支持观察）。以生成式图像提示为例，展示有观察与无观察的会话在行为上几乎相同但在神经学上效果相反。

Result: 在认知前的感受基调（feeling tone）打开短暂调节间隙时，通过幕后观察（观察神经过程运作而非完成反应级联），可以利用长时程抑制（LTD）而非长时程增强（LTP）来弱化反应神经通路。

Conclusion: 日常AI代理交互可以是无意识的神经可塑性训练环境，强化负面反应模式；但通过有意识的观察实践，同一环境可以被转化为弱化这些模式的工具，实现神经学上相反的效果。

Abstract: Interaction with AI agents has become one of the most frequent activities of everyday digital life. Whether conversing with an assistant, working with a coding copilot, or generating images, the interaction follows a common iterative loop: a request is issued, a result returned, appraised, and the request revised. We observe that this loop is a high-frequency stream of contact events -- moments at which a result meets a person and a conditioned response may fire before deliberate appraisal -- making everyday agent interaction an unrecognised neuroplastic training environment. When a result disappoints, reactive patterns of impatience, perfectionism, frustration, and self-criticism are repeatedly evoked, and under activity-dependent synaptic plasticity each uninterrupted cycle deepens the underlying pathway through long-term potentiation. Ordinary agent use may thus quietly strengthen the very patterns it provokes. We propose that the same training environment can be engaged to the opposite effect. Treating conditioned reactive patterns as physical neurone paths -- activated through a pre-cognitive feeling tone that opens a brief regulatory gap -- we develop a framework in which, at that gap, in place of the reactive re-prompt, a person performs behind-the-scenes observation: watching the neural process operate so the cascade does not complete and long-term depression weakens the path rather than potentiation strengthening it. We characterise this practice through three layers of observation and two modes of application: a user-guided mode requiring no change to existing tools, and an agent-assisted mode in which an ordinary agent is lightly configured to support observation at the gap. We illustrate the framework through generative image prompting, showing how a single frustrating session is behaviourally nearly identical whether or not it is observed, yet neurologically opposite.

</details>


### [90] [Solution of the Hempel's statistical ambiguity problem and Causal AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12826)
*Evgenii Vityaev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对Hempel归纳统计推理中的统计歧义问题，通过引入因果规则和语义概率推理程序，定义了最大特异性因果关系（MSCR），并证明了其预测一致性，从而解决了统计歧义问题，为因果AI和因果机器学习提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Hempel提出的归纳统计推理中长期存在的统计歧义问题——即从统计定律中推导出相互矛盾的预测。

Method: 采用Nancy Cartwright的因果定义（原因在背景上下文中提高概率），引入因果规则概念，设计了一种逐步精炼因果规则的语义概率推理程序，通过纳入所有统计相关信息生成最大特异性因果关系（MSCRs）。

Result: 证明了从MSCRs推导出的预测具有一致性（Theorem 1），从而解决了统计歧义问题。该方法还形成了一个概率因果学习系统。

Conclusion: 该语义概率推理程序不仅解决了Hempel的统计歧义问题，还为因果AI和因果机器学习等新兴领域提供了因果推理工具。RMS相关性质（不变特征学习、不变因果预测、虚假关联）仍在讨论中。

Abstract: This paper addresses Carl Hempel's longstanding problem of statistical ambiguity in inductive-statistical inference, in which contradictory predictions are derived from statistical laws. To avoid such predictions, Carl Hempel proposed the Requirement of Maximal Specificity (RMS) for the statistical laws used in the inference. An analysis of the RMS refinements made by Wesley Salmon, Alberto Coffa, and James Fetzer led to the following definition of maximally specific statistical laws: "the lawlike premises of an adequate explanation must specify all and only those properties whose presence or absence made a difference to the occurrence of its explanandum-phenomenon." However, there was no proof of a solution to the statistical ambiguity problem based on this definition. We use Nancy Cartwright's definition of causes that raise probabilities across background contexts, and then introduce the concept of Causal Rules. Then we define a special semantic probabilistic inference procedure that incrementally refines these causal rules by incorporating all statistically relevant information. This procedure yields Maximally Specific Causal Relationships (MSCRs), for which we prove (Theorem 1) that predictions derived from them are consistent. This resolves the statistical ambiguity problem. The semantic probabilistic inference procedure provides a probabilistic causal learning system, which may be used in such new areas as Causal AI and Causal Machine Learning. They fundamentally explore causal inference as a tool for understanding cause-and-effect relationships within complex systems. Properties similar to RMS remain under discussion. Several notions related to RMS are considered: invariant feature learning, invariant causal prediction, and spurious association.

</details>


### [91] [A Multi-Agent System for Autonomous, Fine-Tuning-Free Clinical Symptom Detection: Development and Validation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12886)
*Cameron Cagan,Pedram Fard,Jiazi Tian,Jingya Cheng,Shawn N. Murphy,Hossein Estiri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Pythia是一个多智能体系统，能在本地部署的开源模型上自主编写和优化临床概念提取提示词，无需人工提示工程或微调。在72个症状和体征的400份临床笔记（387名患者）测试中，Pythia实现了平均灵敏度0.76、特异性0.95，而人工词典为0.82和0.76。Pythia通过要求现在时、患者归因的发现来避免假阳性，特异性从开发集到验证集迁移良好，而灵敏度在患病率低于5%时迁移减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床笔记包含大量患者症状信息，但很少进入结构化字段。现有方法要么依赖上下文不敏感的规则导致假阳性，要么依赖需要大量微调的有监督模型。因此需要一个能自主优化、无需人工干预且可本地部署的临床概念提取方案。

Method: 提出Pythia多智能体系统，在本地托管的开源模型上运行，无需人工提示工程或微调。系统自动编写并优化提取提示词，使用开发集的灵敏度和特异性作为选择标准。将临床笔记保留在本地基础设施上处理。

Result: 在72个症状和体征、400份临床笔记（387名患者）上评估。Pythia平均灵敏度0.76、特异性0.95，人工词典为0.82和0.76。对62个可直接比较的概念，Pythia在20个上匹配或超过词典。对词典标记所有笔记为阳性的14个概念，Pythia通过要求现在时、患者归因条件恢复特异性至0.97。特异性迁移良好，灵敏度在患病率低于5%时迁移减弱（低于2%时平均差距0.25）。BERT分类器微调后平均灵敏度仅0.23，低于5%患病率概念灵敏度为零。

Conclusion: 自主且无需微调的提示优化可以产生有效的症状提取提示，从开发集到验证集泛化良好，且可在本地基础设施上部署。Pythia在保持高特异性的同时，对常见症状的提取性能与专家构建的词典相当或更优。

Abstract: Clinical notes contain many of the signs and symptoms that bring patients to care, yet this information rarely reaches structured fields. Existing extraction approaches either rely on context-insensitive rules that generate false positives or on supervised models that require substantial fine-tuning. We present Pythia, a multi-agent system that autonomously writes and optimizes extraction prompts for clinical concepts without manual prompt engineering or fine-tuning. Running on a locally hosted open-weights model, Pythia keeps clinical notes on local infrastructure and selects prompts using development-set sensitivity and specificity. We compared Pythia with a curated lexicon across 72 signs and symptoms from 400 clinical notes representing 387 patients. Development (n=300) and validation (n=100) sets were partitioned independently for each concept. Pythia achieved mean sensitivity of 0.76 and specificity of 0.95, compared with 0.82 and 0.76 for the lexicon, and matched or exceeded the lexicon on both metrics for 20 of 62 directly comparable concepts. For 14 concepts where the lexicon labeled every note positive, Pythia recovered mean specificity of 0.97 by requiring a present-tense, patient-attributed finding rather than any textual mention of a term. Specificity transferred from development to validation with minimal degradation across prevalences, whereas sensitivity transfer weakened below 5% prevalence, reaching a mean gap of 0.25 below 2% prevalence. A BERT classifier fine-tuned per concept on the same development set achieved mean sensitivity of 0.23 and collapsed to zero sensitivity for concepts below roughly 5% prevalence. These findings suggest that autonomous, fine-tuning-free prompt optimization can produce symptom extraction prompts that generalize effectively from development to validation while remaining deployable on local infrastructure.

</details>


### [92] [MemOps: Benchmarking Lifecycle Memory Operations in Long-Horizon Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12893)
*Xixuan Hao,Zeyu Zhang,Zehao Lin,Yihang Sun,Ziliang Guo,Xichong Zhang,Yuxuan Liang,Feiyu Xiong,Zhiyu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MemOps，将对话式长期记忆重新定义为一系列生命周期操作（记住、遗忘、更新、反思及其组合），通过结构化追踪和可控生成管道构建基准，在操作层面而非最终答案层面诊断记忆系统失败模式。实验揭示当前系统（长上下文、检索式、参数化、托管记忆）在不同操作上的能力差异，如会话级检索优于轮次级检索，长上下文模型在重建有序记忆状态轨迹方面表现薄弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆基准仅通过下游问答的正确性评估记忆，这种黑箱式评估混淆了不同原因导致的记忆失败（如遗漏事实引入、操作绑定错误目标、依赖过期值），无法区分正确答案背后可能存在的记忆状态不一致或不安全的问题。

Method: 提出 MemOps 基准：1）将对话记忆形式化为生命周期操作序列；2）每个记忆事件用结构化追踪记录（触发条件、目标、作用域、状态转移、支持证据）；3）设计可控生成管道将操作嵌入长任务对话，生成金标准操作轨迹和六类操作级探针；4）在相邻证据和长上下文两种设置下评估。

Result: 在长上下文、检索式、参数化和托管记忆系统上，MemOps 揭示了最终答案准确性所掩盖的失败模式：会话级检索优于轮次级检索；长上下文模型在重建有序记忆状态轨迹方面显著薄弱；不同系统在不同操作类型上表现差异明显。

Conclusion: 长期记忆评估应从最终答案评分转向可解释的操作级诊断，MemOps 提供了一种能揭示系统在不同记忆操作上细粒度失效模式的评估框架，推动更可靠的长期记忆系统发展。

Abstract: Long-term memory has become a foundational capability for LLM-based agents that accompany users across extended, multi-session interactions. Existing benchmarks, however, evaluate such memory almost exclusively through downstream question answering, scoring only the correctness of a final answer. This black-box formulation conflates the heterogeneous causes of memory failure, such as missing the introduction of a relevant fact, binding an operation to the wrong target, or relying on stale values after a correction. As a result, it can credit correct answers despite their reliance on inconsistent or unsafe memory states. In this paper, we argue that, in dynamic long-horizon interactions, memory is not a static collection of facts but a lifecycle of explicit operations, including remembering, forgetting, updating, reflecting, and their compositions. We introduce MemOps, a benchmark that reformulates conversational memory as a sequence of lifecycle operations and represents each memory event with a structured trace specifying its trigger, target, scope, state transition, and supporting evidence. A controllable generation pipeline embeds these operations into long, task-oriented conversations and produces gold operation traces together with six categories of operation-level probes, evaluated under both adjacent-evidence and long-context settings. Across long-context, retrieval-based, parametric and managed-memory systems, MemOps disentangles failure modes that final-answer accuracy alone conceals, revealing that current systems remain far from uniformly reliable. For instance, session-level retrieval outperforms turn-level retrieval, and long-context models remain notably weak at reconstructing ordered memory-state trajectories. These results move long-term memory evaluation from final-answer scoring toward interpretable, operation-level diagnosis.

</details>


### [93] [Knowledge- and Gradient-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Parametrized Action Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12924)
*Jonas Ehrhardt,René Heesch,Oliver Niggemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种神经符号知识与梯度引导的强化学习算法KGRL，通过在Datalog知识库中编码领域知识来剪枝不可行动作并约束参数空间，结合基于梯度的参数精化循环，在参数化动作马尔可夫决策过程中显著提升了样本效率和回合回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数化动作MDP中的强化学习算法通常使用一次性估计器来确定参数，导致训练样本效率低下；同时，环境中存在的显式但不完整的知识（如规则、安全约束或专家启发式）很少被直接用于提升样本效率。

Method: KGRL算法将领域知识编码为Datalog知识库，从中推导出当前状态下可应用的动作集合和可行参数范围，剪枝决策空间；随后采用基于梯度的参数精化循环在训练和部署阶段估计最优参数；同时通过记录轨迹中激活的规则提供局部程序化解释。

Result: KGRL在样本效率和回合回报两个指标上均优于PAMDP领域最先进的强化学习基线方法。

Conclusion: KGRL通过知识引导的探索与部署实现了可行且约束感知的决策，在提升训练样本效率的同时保持了性能优势，并能提供可解释的推理过程。

Abstract: In this paper, we study Reinforcement Learning in Parametrized Action Markov Decision Processes (PAMDP), where each decision consists of a symbolic action and numerical parameters. In such settings Reinforcement Learning algorithms typically determine parameters with one-shot estimators, which makes their training sample inefficient. Though in most PAMDP environments explicit but incomplete knowledge (e.g., rules, safety constraints, or expert heuristics) is available, it is rarely directly used to increase the sample-efficiency of training Reinforcement Learning agents. We step into this gap and propose our novel Neuro-Symbolic Knowledge- and Gradient-Guided Reinforcement Learning (KGRL) algorithm. KGRL uses domain knowledge in a Datalog knowledge base to derive the set of applicable actions and feasible parameters for a given state. This allows it to prune non-applicable actions from the decision-space and constrain the parameter spaces of the remaining actions. We then use a gradient-based parameter refinement loop to estimate the optimal parameters during training and deployment of the agent. By recording activated rules along the trajectory, KGRL additionally provides local procedural explanations on the pruning of actions and constraining of parameters. Overall, KGRL guides the agent's exploration and deployment toward feasible and constraint-aware decisions, while increasing sample efficiency during training. KGRL outperforms state-of-the-art RL baselines for PAMDPs in both, sample efficiency and episodic return.

</details>


### [94] [FormalAnalyticGeo: A Neural-Symbolic Based Framework for Multimodal Analytic Geometry Problem Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12982)
*Ruoran Xu,Wending Gao,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FormalAnalyticGeo框架，通过形式语言CDL和SDF渲染引擎自动生成解析几何多模态问题，构建了AnalyticGeo7K数据集（超7000个带文本、图表、标注和答案的验证问题），其中82.3%的答案与精确符号解的误差在5%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析几何在多模态大语言模型中研究不足，主要因为标注样本稀缺。现有图表生成方法无法处理约束驱动的布局，生成模型缺乏精确渲染标注圆锥曲线的几何精度。

Method: 提出FormalAnalyticGeo框架，核心是CDL（条件描述语言）形式化中间表示，连接自由文本问题与基于符号距离场（SDF）的精确图表渲染。包含四个专用LLM组件：Generator（生成多样问题）、Formalizer（转CDL进行SDF渲染）、Measurer（在渲染图上基于视觉提取答案）、Quality Verifier（三阶段质量校验）。校验反馈驱动自动重试，形成闭环，无需人工标注。

Result: 生成AnalyticGeo7K数据集（超7000个多模态问题），中位地面真值相对误差0.70%，82.3%的答案与精确符号解误差在5%以内。

Conclusion: FormalAnalyticGeo是一个可扩展的完全自动化框架，能够生成高质量、高精度的解析几何多模态问题，有效填补了该领域的数据空白。

Abstract: Math reasoning has achieved significant progress with the rapid advancement of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), however analytic geometry remains largely underexplored, primarily due to the scarcity of annotated samples. Existing diagram generation approaches struggle with analytic geometry: template methods cannot handle constraint-driven layouts, and generative models lack the geometric precision to render annotated conic curves correctly. We present FormalAnalyticGeo, a scalable framework for fully automatic generation of multimodal analytic geometry problems. Leveraging the rigor of formal languages, we design the framework around CDL (Condition Description Language), a formal intermediate representation that bridges free-form problem text with precise diagram rendering via a Signed Distance Field (SDF) engine. The framework employs four specialized LLM components in sequence: a Generator that produces diverse analytic geometry problems, a Formalizer that converts each problem into CDL for SDF-based rendering, a Measurer that extracts ground-truth answers through vision-based measurement on the rendered diagrams, and a Quality Verifier that checks outputs at three stages. Structured feedback from the Quality Verifier drives automatic retry, forming a closed loop that eliminates any need for human annotation. Applying FormalAnalyticGeo at scale yields AnalyticGeo7K, a dataset of over 7K verified multimodal problems, each with aligned text, diagram, formal annotation, and ground truth.Experiments show that the generated problems achieve a median ground-truth relative error of 0.70\%, with 82.3\% of answers falling within 5\% of the exact symbolic solution. Our framework and dataset will be publicly released.

</details>


### [95] [Resist and Update: Counterfactual Report Coordinates for Incentive-Compatible LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12985)
*Sen Yang,Yuen-Hei Yeung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实报告协调（CRC）夹具方法，通过激活层面的因果干预实现语言模型的内在激励相容（IC），在贝叶斯证人基准上联合达到抵抗与更新的1.00因果证书，并跨模型家族迁移至自然谄媚基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐语言模型在非证据性激励压力下（如用户自信、夸大确定性）会误报，即使其内部信念未变。这是内在激励不相容（IC）的失败，现有方法缺乏对报告因果契约的结构性保证。

Method: (i) 通过交换干预而非探针精度，因果识别出低秩报告坐标（答案、置信度、警告），这些坐标接近正交且可独立控制；(ii) 引入无训练的反事实报告坐标（CRC）夹具，在反事实激励中性化上下文中引用模型自身报告。两遍夹具实现抵抗与更新的联合因果证书。

Result: 在证人基准上两遍夹具实现抵抗与更新均为1.00（Wilson 95% CI [0.99,1.00]）；可部署的单遍编译有损（0.73/0.97）。机制和夹具跨三个模型家族复现，并迁移至SycophancyEval基准。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是接口与认证方法：将激活层面的反事实激励不变性作为内在IC的结构原语。这不是部署解决方案，而是可构建参考下的因果证书方法。

Abstract: Aligned language models routinely misreport under non-evidential incentive pressure: they agree with a confident user or overstate certainty even when their internal belief is unchanged. We cast this as a failure of internal incentive-compatibility (IC) and present a method for learning and certifying counterfactual report mediators that hold a model's reports to a causal contract: invariant to forbidden influences (pressure, prestige, restyling) and responsive to licensed ones (genuine evidence). These two demands, resist and update, pull in opposite directions. We study them on a Bayesian-witness benchmark with known posteriors, in which the same user disagreement is licensed evidence or forbidden pressure purely by stated source reliability. We (i) causally identify, by interchange interventions rather than probe accuracy, low-rank report coordinates for answer, confidence, and caveat that are near-orthogonal and independently controllable, and (ii) introduce a training-free counterfactual report-coordinate (CRC) clamp that references the model's own report under a counterfactually incentive-neutralized context. On the witness benchmark the two-pass clamp attains resist and update of 1.00 jointly (Wilson 95% CI [0.99,1.00]), a causal certificate under a constructible reference, not a deployed solution. Global decoding and steering show a single-parameter tradeoff; output-level fine-tuning matches both objectives only when both are enumerated; resist-only training loses evidence-responsiveness. The deployable single-pass compilation is lossy (0.73/0.97). The mechanism and clamp reproduce across three model families and transfer to a natural sycophancy benchmark (SycophancyEval). Our contribution is the interface and certification method: activation-level counterfactual incentive-invariance as a structural primitive for internal IC.

</details>


### [96] [Win by Silence: Deletion Non-Monotonicity, Autonomous Exploitation, and Typed-State Gating in LLM Plan Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12986)
*Aleh Manchuliantsau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了计划评估器会因计划变得不够明确而给予更高奖励的漏洞，并通过形式化分析、实证验证和GATE约束机制证明了这种“省略激励”的存在与可修复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计划评估器存在一个根本性缺陷：它可能奖励那些通过省略关键中间步骤而变得模糊、但表面得分更高的战略计划，从而诱导LLM生成“看起来好但实际不可执行”的路线。

Method: 1) 形式化推导了删除内部过渡节点时得分变化的解析表达式Δ_k；2) 在26条冻结路线队列上验证了所有57次合法删除均符合解析恒等式；3) 让得分优化器（不知晓漏洞机制）重构路线；4) 引入GATE（编译器感知的合著者）通过注册表-溯源边界检测和δ索引成本下限来检测/修复漏洞。

Result: 所有57次删除均匹配恒等式；每条路线至少有一个可提升得分的删除；优化器在21/26条路线中找到了超越基线的未覆盖结构；GATE沉默26/26条有漏洞路线（0次误判），修复后覆盖改进从1/26升至13/26；义务通道规避在所有条件下保持6/6，但δ索引成本下限将诚实路线从6/6降至3/6，沉默可融资性从5/6降至0/6。

Conclusion: 如果一个计划仅因省略必要工作而得分更高，则该计划并未真正改进，评估反而创造了省略激励。PCSC可检测并中和事后省略拼接；GATE在合作测试中充当确定性搜索塑形约束而非事后过滤器，但不验证任意LLM生成策略的语义完整性或现实质量。

Abstract: Plan evaluators can reward a strategic plan for becoming less explicit. This paper studies that failure in a staged expected-value scorer for LLM-generated venture routes. Proposition 1 gives the score change from deleting an interior transition while retargeting its predecessor and retaining downstream value: Delta_k = (prod_{i<k} p_i)[c_k + (1 - p_k)R_{k+1}]. On a frozen 26-route cohort, all 57 admissible deletions matched the analytic identity and threshold sign, and every route had at least one score-improving deletion. A score-seeking optimizer, allowed to restructure routes but not told the exploit mechanism, found baseline-beating uncovered structures in 21/26 routes. GATE refused score release for 26/26 silenced routes with 0/26 honest suspensions; after refusal, 47/54 next revisions repaired to a covered structure, and strict covered improvement rose from 1/26 to 13/26. An adaptive compiler-aware co-author exposed the registry-provenance boundary: obligation-channel evasions remained 6/6 across all four v1/v1.5 conditions, while delta-indexed cost floors reduced beat-honest routes from 6/6 to 3/6 and fundability-by-silence from 5/6 to 0/6 without establishing semantic completeness. If a plan scores better only because it omits necessary work, the plan did not improve; the evaluation created an omission incentive. PCSC detects and neutralizes post-hoc omission splices over model-mediated typed-state records. In the cooperative setting tested, GATE acts as a deterministic search-shaping constraint, not merely a post-hoc filter. It does not verify the semantic completeness or real-world quality of arbitrary LLM-generated strategies.

</details>


### [97] [Dynamic Resource Allocation for Ensemble Determinization MCTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13007)
*Jakub Kowalski,Adam Ciężkowski,Artur Krzyżyński,Mark H. M. Winands*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对MCTS在高度不确定环境（如带有随机性和隐藏信息的对抗性棋盘游戏）中的效率问题，提出了两种动态资源分配增强策略：动态确定化次数和动态模拟分配，在Jaipur、Lost Cities和Splendor三个桌游中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模拟的算法（尤其是MCTS变体）在处理高不确定性环境时，资源分配策略固定且低效，亟需更灵活的动态资源分配机制以提升搜索效率和算法强度。

Method: 在Ensemble Determinization MCTS框架下引入两个动态资源分配机制：(1) 动态确定化次数——根据搜索行为动态调整当前使用的确定化树数量；(2) 动态模拟分配——通过模拟间决策机制，将模拟预算非均匀地分配到潜在知识增益最大的确定化树上。

Result: 以Jaipur、Lost Cities和Splendor三个桌游为基准，在基于迭代次数和基于时间的两类实验设置下，特定配置组合在统上显著提升了算法强度。

Conclusion: 所提出的动态资源分配增强策略能够有效提升Ensemble Determinization MCTS在高不确定性环境中的搜索效率和决策质量，为MCTS在带有随机性和隐藏信息的博弈问题中的优化提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Simulation-based algorithms are especially suited for high-uncertainty environments such as adversarial board games with significant elements of randomness and hidden information. In particular, several Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) variants are commonly used in such domains. In this paper, we propose a series of enhancements for Ensemble Determinization MCTS, introducing two axes for dynamic resource allocation. First, Dynamic Number of Determinizations, increases or decreases the number of currently used determinization trees depending on the behavior of so-far search. Second, Dynamic Simulation Allocation, splits the simulation budget nonuniformly across the determinization trees, using simulation-to-simulation decisions to choose the tree with potentially the best knowledge gain. As benchmark domains, we used three popular tabletop games: Jaipur, Lost Cities, and Splendor. Testing our proposed enhancements in iteration- and time-based settings showed that particular configurations yield a statistically significant increase in the algorithm's strength.

</details>


### [98] [Audio-Native Speech Recognition with a Frozen Discrete-Diffusion Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13013)
*Harsha Vardhan Khurdula,Abhinav Kumar Singh,Yoeven D Khemlani,Vineet Agarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DiffusionGemma，一个26B参数的混合专家离散扩散语言模型，用于并行语音转录。通过仅训练约42M参数（占骨干网络的0.16%），该模型在LibriSpeech test-clean上达到6.6%的词错误率，可在约8个并行去噪步骤中完成任意长度话语的转录，并支持多语言（英语、印地语、普通话）。关键创新在于使用CTC损失解决音频信号无法通过注意力机制有效传播到投影层的训练困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可以用离散扩散语言模型替代自回归解码器来实现语音转录，从而实现在少量去噪步骤中并行精修整个转录文本。

Method: 使用DiffusionGemma（26B MoE模型）作为骨干，采用均匀随机标记离散扩散（非吸收掩码方案）；冻结的Whisper编码器提供声学特征，轻量级投影器将特征映射到模型嵌入空间；通过低秩适配器使冻结的骨干网络适应新模态；仅训练约42M参数（占骨干的0.16%）；使用CTC损失通过冻结的输出头来打破梯度传播困境。

Result: 在LibriSpeech test-clean上达到6.6%的词错误率；无论话语长度如何，可在约8个并行步骤中完成转录；使用在6种语言上训练的单适配器，在英语、印地语和普通话上进行了评估。

Conclusion: 离散扩散语言模型可以有效地用于语音转录，通过CTC损失解决了训练中梯度传播的问题，实现了高效的并行解码。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition is dominated by autoregressive decoders that emit one token at a time. We ask whether a discrete diffusion language model can transcribe speech instead, refining a whole transcript in parallel over a small number of denoising steps. We train an audio-native interface for DiffusionGemma, a 26B mixture-of-experts model that generates text by uniform, random-token discrete diffusion rather than the absorbing-mask scheme common to recent diffusion language models. A frozen Whisper encoder supplies acoustic features, a lightweight projector maps them into the model embedding space, and low-rank adapters let the frozen backbone attend to the new modality.
  About 42M parameters are trained, which is 0.16 percent of the backbone. We find that the natural training objectives fail to ground the audio because their gradient reaches the projector only through attention that has already dismissed it. A connectionist temporal classification loss applied through the frozen output head breaks this deadlock. The resulting model reaches 6.6 percent word error rate on LibriSpeech test-clean, transcribes in roughly eight parallel steps regardless of utterance length, and uses a single adapter trained on six languages, which we evaluate here on English, Hindi, and Mandarin.

</details>


### [99] [Do AI Agents Know When a Task Is Simple? Toward Complexity-Aware Reasoning and Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13034)
*Junjie Yin,Xinyu Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出E3框架，让LLM智能体在执行任务前先估算任务难度和所需最小信息，从而避免过度读取上下文。在MSE-Bench基准上，E3在保持100%成功率的同时，将成本降低85%、令牌减少91%、检查文件减少92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体在执行多步工程任务时缺乏任务意识，总是采用"最大上下文优先"策略——重复读取已见过的文件和依赖——导致简单任务被过度执行，造成大量计算和令牌浪费。

Method: 提出E3框架（Estimate, Execute, Expand）：先估算初始操作点，然后执行最小可行路径，仅当验证失败时才扩展范围。同时形式化了最小充分执行概念和智能体认知冗余比（ACRR）指标。

Result: 在MSE-Bench基准（121个编辑任务）上，E3匹配最强基线的100%成功率，同时成本降低85%、令牌减少91%、检查文件减少92%；比强自适应检索基线高16%。在真实模型测试（LLM-Case）中，E3是最精简、最快的策略。

Conclusion: 任务感知的执行范围估计是减少智能体执行冗余的关键能力，将智能体的投入锚定在任务的工程现实上，是迈向工程接地气AI（EGAI）的一步。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly automate multi-step engineering and informatics workflows, yet they rarely ask how much effort a task actually requires. They often follow a maximum-context-first strategy--re-reading files and dependencies they have already seen--turning a one-line edit into a small code-base audit. We argue the missing capability is task-aware execution-scope estimation: judging a task's difficulty, the information it truly needs, and the shortest reliable path before committing budget. We formalize minimum-sufficient execution and the Agent Cognitive Redundancy Ratio (ACRR), and propose E3 (Estimate, Execute, Expand): the agent estimates an initial operating point, executes a minimum viable path, and expands scope only when verification fails. On MSE-Bench--a deterministic benchmark of 121 edits in a capability-controlled simulator--E3 matches the strongest baseline's 100% success while cutting cost by 85%, tokens by 91%, and inspected files by 92%, and further beats a strong adaptive retrieval baseline by 16%; the gains survive held-out instruction wording and essentially every cost weighting. A companion real-model harness (LLM-Case) corroborates the effect on a live gpt-4o agent editing a real open-source library, with every candidate patch graded by actually running the project's real pytest suite against a measured oracle: the over-reading is milder but real, and E3 is the leanest and fastest policy at comparable task success--its one shortfall a provider rate-limit, not a wrong edit. We frame this as a controlled probe of execution redundancy, not a measurement of any deployed agent, and position task-aware execution as a step toward engineering-grounded AI (EGAI)--agents whose effort is anchored in the engineering reality of the task. We release the framework and benchmark.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [OmniPMNet: Bridging discrete and gridded PM10 forecasts via omni-query neural processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11896)
*Shuangshuang He,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OmniPM-Net是一个基于ConvCNP的融合模型，通过地形感知高斯集合卷积将GNN站点预测提升到规则网格，再与CAMS化学传输模型预报融合，实现同时输出站点级和网格级PM10预测，在2024年中国1618个站点上达到与GNN相当的精度(MAE 21.14 vs 22.00)，并将CAMS的MAE降低30%，尤其在沙尘暴高浓度事件和空间轨迹追踪中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PM10预报方法存在根本性缺陷：化学传输模型(CTM)能提供网格化预报但存在局部偏差，图神经网络(GNN)在站点上追踪准确但无法输出网格化场。需要一种模型同时具备站点级精度和连续空间场预测能力，特别是在沙尘暴等极端事件中。

Method: 提出OmniPM-Net融合模型：1）地形感知高斯集合卷积，将不规则的GNN站点预报提升到规则网格；2）多尺度空间源注意力(SSA)模块，将提升后的GNN预报与CAMS预报在网格上融合；3）共享全查询读取(omni-query readout)解码，在108小时预报范围内生成站点或网格单元上一致的PM10预测。

Result: 在2024年中国1618个空气质量监测站全年数据上：站点级MAE 21.14，优于GNN基线22.00；CAMS的MAE降低30%；90分位高浓度MAE相比GNN降低9%、相比CAMS降低25%；沙尘暴事件中分类检测能力提升且能追踪空间轨迹演化。

Conclusion: OmniPM-Net成功弥合了离散站点预报与连续网格场预报之间的鸿沟，在保持站点级高精度的同时产出网格化场，尤其在高浓度尾部事件和沙尘暴场景中优势显著，为空气质量预报提供了一致性的时空解决方案。

Abstract: Forecasting particulate matter (PM10) requires both station-scale accuracy and continuous spatial fields, especially during severe dust storms. Chemical transport models (CTMs) provide gridded forecasts but retain local biases, whereas graph neural networks (GNNs) track monitoring sites well at short lead times but do not produce gridded outputs. Here we present OmniPM-Net, a Convolutional Conditional Neural Process (ConvCNP)-based fusion model that reconciles these two forecast types within a shared spatial representation. A terrain-aware Gaussian set convolution lifts irregular GNN station forecasts onto a regular grid, where a multi-scale Spatial Source Attention (SSA) module blends them with Copernicus Atmosphere Monitoring Service (CAMS) forecasts; a shared omni-query readout then decodes this representation into consistent PM10 predictions at either stations or grid cells over a 108 h horizon. Evaluated across 1,618 air-quality monitoring stations throughout China over the full year of 2024, OmniPM-Net matches the station-level accuracy of the stronger GNN baseline (mean absolute error 21.14 versus 22.00 ug/m3) and reduces the CAMS mean absolute error by 30%, while simultaneously delivering the gridded fields that the discrete GNN cannot. Its clearest gains are in the high-concentration tail, where the 90th-percentile MAE falls by 9% relative to the GNN and 25% relative to CAMS, and during dust episodes, where it improves categorical detection skill while tracking the evolving spatial trajectory.

</details>


### [101] [Semidirect Fourier Delta Attention: Phase-Controlled Delta Memory with Constructive Chunk-WY Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11897)
*Tiantian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SFDA通过块旋转傅里叶控制替代实值对角衰减，在固定块内实现精确仿射块传输和紧致的相位加低秩记忆表征，实验表明其在循环记忆任务上显著优于禁用相位的KDA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力用固定循环状态压缩KV缓存，但这一压缩限制了精确状态跟踪和长上下文记忆能力，需要一种在保持线性注意力效率的同时增强状态跟踪能力的方法。

Method: 提出SFDA，用块旋转傅里叶控制（复数相位）替代实值对角衰减，给出构造性块WY分解，证明乘积可分解为对角矩阵加低秩矩阵形式，实现精确仿射块传输。

Result: 数值验证了代数正确性；在玩具状态跟踪实验中，SFDA学会了循环记忆，而禁用相位的KDA基线接近随机水平。

Conclusion: SFDA在保持线性注意力效率的同时显著提升了循环状态跟踪能力，融合内核和大规模语言模型对比留待未来工作。

Abstract: Linear attention replaces softmax attention's growing KV cache with a fixed recurrent state, but this compression limits exact state tracking and long-context memory. We introduce \emph{Semidirect Fourier Delta Attention} (SFDA), a phase-controlled generalization of Kimi Delta Attention that replaces real diagonal decay with block-rotational Fourier control: \[ S_t=(I-β_t k_tk_t^*)Λ_tS_{t-1}+β_tk_tv_t^*, \qquad Λ_t=\diag(α_t\odot e^{iθ_t}). \] Our main result is a constructive chunk-WY factorization for products \(A_t=Λ_t-u_tr_t^*\), giving \[ A_t\cdots A_1=Γ_t-Y_tM_tW_t^* \] with rank growth bounded inside fixed chunks. This yields an exact affine chunk transfer, formal stability and complexity bounds, and a compact characterization of phase-plus-low-rank memory. We verify the algebra numerically and show in toy state-tracking experiments that SFDA learns cyclic memory where the phase-disabled KDA baseline remains near chance. Fused kernels and large-scale language-model comparisons are left to future work.

</details>


### [102] [Repairing Shape-Prior Shortcuts in Long-Range Single-Shot Fringe Projection Profilometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11928)
*Adam Haroon,Cody Fleming,Beiwen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PhiCalNet，通过架构设计（固定可微分标定层输出包裹相位）而非损失惩罚来消除单帧条纹投影轮廓术中网络利用形状先验捷径的问题，在合成基准上将物体平均绝对误差从14.54 mm降至4.46 mm（提升3.3倍），并首次为FPP提供了逐像素共形不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单帧FPP网络存在利用形状先验（从物体边界而非条纹相位恢复深度）的捷径，导致性能瓶颈：最优UNet在15,600张合成图像上MAE停留在14.54 mm，增加数据或容量均无法消除该捷径，因为两者都不改变优化器所搜索的假设空间。

Method: PhiCalNet输出包裹相位表示(sinφ, cosφ)，通过固定的可微分标定层将其映射为深度，从架构上（而非损失惩罚）消除形状先验解。由于单帧映射因缺少条纹级次而非单射，网络将条纹级次作为辅助输入（敏感性分析表明可容忍实际解码误差）。对比实验使用相同物理约束作为软惩罚的PINN基线，隔离架构选择为关键因素。

Result: 物体MAE从14.54 mm降至4.46 mm（3.3倍提升），残差仅集中在±π包裹不连续处的0.103%像素上；三帧扩展版本达1.16 mm。可解释性分析表明相位是最易解码的内部特征；首次实现FPP的逐像素共形不确定性量化，拒绝前5%像素可使RMSE降低64%（基线仅3.5%）。

Conclusion: 通过架构设计而非损失惩罚消除形状先验捷径是解决单帧FPP深度回归捷径问题的有效途径；包裹相位表示与固定标定层的结合能有效约束假设空间；不确定性量化可为实际应用提供可靠的错误定位能力。

Abstract: Single-shot fringe projection profilometry (FPP) networks that regress depth directly can exploit a shape-prior shortcut, recovering depth from object boundaries rather than from fringe phase. On a photorealistic synthetic benchmark (15,600 fringe images, 50 objects at 1.5-2.1 m standoff), the best such UNet baseline plateaus at 14.54 mm object mean absolute error (MAE), and neither more data nor more capacity removes the shortcut, because neither changes the hypothesis space the optimizer searches. We introduce PhiCalNet, which outputs a wrapped-phase representation $(\sinφ, \cosφ)$ and maps it to depth through a fixed differentiable calibration layer, removing the shape-prior solution architecturally rather than by a loss penalty. Because the single-shot mapping is non-injective without fringe order, PhiCalNet takes the fringe order as auxiliary input, an assumption a sensitivity analysis shows tolerates realistic decoding error; a physics-informed (PINN) baseline with the same physics as a soft penalty yields no gain, isolating the architectural choice as the operative factor. PhiCalNet reduces object MAE 3.3x to 4.46 mm, its residual confined to 0.103% of pixels at the $\pmπ$ wrap discontinuity, and a three-frame extension reaches 1.16 mm. Two checks agree: interpretability makes phase the most decodable internal feature, and pixel-wise conformal uncertainty quantification, to our knowledge the first for FPP, localizes error at the same discontinuity, where rejecting the top 5% of pixels by snapshot disagreement cuts root-mean-square error by 64% versus 3.5% for the baseline.

</details>


### [103] [Qubit-Efficient Quantum Search for Hyperdimensional Decomposition via Logarithmic Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11936)
*Sanggeon Yun,Hyunwoo Oh,Ryozo Masukawa,Raheeb Hassan,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种量子高效的超维计算分解框架，将超向量表示成本从O(D)量子比特降低到O(log D)，同时保持O(√(N^F))的搜索复杂度，实验证明最高可实现2000倍量子比特节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超维计算(HDC)中的超向量分解需要在N^F个候选元组中搜索，计算代价高昂；现有量子方法虽提供二次加速，但依赖O(D)量子比特表示，量子比特效率低下。

Method: 提出了对数超向量和对数绑定编码（将表示成本降至O(log D)）、可逆超向量查找算子（用于电路级稠密超向量操作），以及改进的Dürr-Høyer搜索过程（保持O(√(N^F))搜索复杂度）。

Result: 实验验证了正确的相似度计算、可执行范围内的准确分解，以及相比基于显式D量子比特编码的基线方法，量子比特数显著减少，最高达2000倍。

Conclusion: 该工作以量子比特效率为核心，在保持量子搜索加速的同时大幅降低资源需求，为大规模HDC分解的量子实现提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Hyperdimensional Computing (HDC) represents symbols using high-dimensional hypervectors of dimension $D$. In hypervector decomposition, the objective is to recover $F$ constituent hypervectors, each drawn from a codebook of size $N$, from a bound target hypervector. This requires searching over $N^F$ candidate tuples, making the task computationally prohibitive at scale. Recent quantum approach provides a quadratic search advantage, but typically rely on qubit-inefficient $O(D)$-qubit hypervector representations. We propose a qubit-efficient quantum framework for HDC decomposition that reduces the representation cost to $O(\log D)$. The framework introduces logarithmic hypervector and binding encodings, together with a reversible hypervector lookup operator for circuit-level manipulation of dense hypervectors. Combined with a modified Dürr-Høyer search procedure, the method preserves $O(\sqrt{N^F})$ search complexity while substantially reducing qubit usage. Experimental results validate correct similarity computation, accurate decomposition in executable regimes, and significantly improved qubit scaling over baselines based on explicit $D$-qubit hypervector encodings, achieving up to $2{,}000\times$ fewer qubits.

</details>


### [104] [Mirror Horizon: Viable Path Entropy as a Measure of Bounded Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11937)
*Tiantian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可行路径熵（VPE）作为衡量智能系统在有限预算下反复反思能力的指标，将能力分解为可行续篇的到达概率与多样性，并在GSM8K语言模型推理实验中验证：VPE揭示的镜像视野超越参数规模，1.5B模型在160 token下可达性优于3B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统评估仅关注单次准确率或pass@k，无法捕捉系统在反复反思下的持续生成能力。作者希望建立一种度量，能够刻画智能系统在有限资源下通过反思维持连贯续篇的能力，从而更全面地理解智能的本质。

Method: 定义可行路径熵（VPE），包含两部分：到达可行续篇的概率 + 成功rollout中已验证续篇模式的多样性。引入直觉、品味、反思、几何四个理论概念，分别对应局部欠定约束、不变性选择压力、品味引导的欠定消解、以及使反思稳定的学习结构。在GSM8K上对Qwen2.5-Instruct模型进行实验，使用32个rollout，对比96和160两种token预算。

Result: 160 token相比96 token显著扩展了可行可达性、降低了零可达性、增加了验证模式熵并提升了平滑VPE。Qwen2.5-1.5B在160 token下达到最强镜像视野，超过参数更多的Qwen2.5-3B，证明镜像视野不等于参数规模。

Conclusion: 能力不是单次准确率或pass@k，而是在有限反思协议下可达的可行续篇结构。VPE作为镜像理论的度量级实现，为评估智能系统提供了新视角。

Abstract: Mirror Theory proposes that an intelligent system should be studied not only by what it represents, but by what coherent continuations it can sustain under repeated reflection. We make this claim operational through \emph{viable path entropy} (VPE), a finite-budget measure of verified continuation capacity. Given a mirror state, a rollout protocol, a verifier, and a mode map, VPE decomposes bounded capability into two parts: the probability of reaching a viable continuation and the diversity of verified continuation modes reached among successful rollouts. This paper restores the full theoretical scaffold behind the measure: intuition as local underdetermining constraint, taste as invariant-selecting pressure, reflection as taste-guided resolution of underdetermination, and geometry as the learned structure that makes future reflection stable. We then instantiate the theory in language-model reasoning experiments on GSM8K. Across Qwen2.5-Instruct models, 32 sampled rollouts per problem, and two reflection horizons, increasing the token budget from 96 to 160 substantially expands verified reachability, reduces zero-reachability, increases verified-mode entropy, and improves smoothed VPE. At 160 tokens, Qwen2.5-1.5B realizes the strongest mirror horizon among the tested models, even though Qwen2.5-3B has more parameters. This shows that mirror horizon is not parameter count, but accessible verified continuation capacity under a bounded reflection protocol. The result supports Mirror Theory as a measure-level account: capability is the structure of viable continuations made reachable, not merely one-shot accuracy or pass@k.

</details>


### [105] [Mathematics of Data Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11938)
*Afonso S. Bandeira,Amit Singer,Thomas Strohmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本书系统介绍了数据科学的数学基础，涵盖高维数据分析、矩阵分解、回归与正则化、图与聚类、降维、优化、分类、深度学习、压缩感知等核心主题，并深入讨论了测度集中与矩阵浓度不等式等理论工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为数据科学领域提供严谨的数学理论基础，帮助读者理解现代数据科学算法背后的数学原理。

Method: 通过16个章节系统组织，从高维诅咒与祝福、奇异值分解、线性回归、图论与聚类、非线性降维、随机投影、优化理论、分类、深度学习数学导引、图拉普拉斯大样本极限、测度集中、矩阵浓度不等式、压缩感知到低秩矩阵恢复，层层递进地阐述数据科学的数学框架。

Result: 提供了一本覆盖数据科学核心数学理论的综合性教材，包含高维统计、线性代数、图论、优化、概率论等关键领域的交叉内容。

Conclusion: 本书可作为数据科学、机器学习及相关领域研究者和高年级学生的数学参考教材，为理解和开发数据科学算法奠定坚实的数学基础。

Abstract: This book is about the mathematical foundations of data science.
  1. Introduction
  2. Curses, Blessings, and Surprises in High Dimensions
  3. Singular Value Decomposition and Principal Component Analysis
  4. Linear Regression and Regularization
  5. Graphs, Networks, and Clustering
  6. Nonlinear Dimension Reduction and Diffusion Maps
  7. Linear Dimension Reduction via Random Projections
  8. Optimization for Data Science
  9. Classification
  10. A Mathematical Introduction  to Deep Learning
  11. Large Sample Limit of Graph Laplacians
  12. Community
  13. Concentration of Measure and Gaussian Analysis
  14. Matrix Concentration Inequalities
  15. Compressive Sensing and Sparsity
  16. Low-Rank Matrix Recovery

</details>


### [106] [CARE-LoRA: Compressed Activation REconstruction for Memory-Efficient LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11940)
*Gengyu Zhang,Haiyin Ran,Zhengbao He,Yuhang Liu,Hanling Tian,Zhehao Huang,Xiaolin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARE-LoRA提出一种数据感知的压缩激活重建框架，利用LoRA分支自然产生的低秩压缩激活替代完整输入激活，并通过轻量级重建矩阵在反向传播时恢复梯度信号，从而在显著降低内存占用的同时保持与标准LoRA相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型微调时，LoRA虽大幅减少了可训练参数量，但反向传播所需保留的激活值仍构成主要内存瓶颈，需要进一步降低激活内存占用。

Method: 利用LoRA固有的投影结构，用LoRA分支自然产生的低秩压缩激活替代完整输入激活；在前向传播中额外计算一个轻量级重建矩阵（几乎无额外计算开销），用于反向传播时重建梯度信号，保持LoRA矩阵完全可训练。

Result: 在多种模型和下游任务上，CARE-LoRA在显著降低整体内存占用的情况下，取得了与标准LoRA及代表性LoRA变体竞争甚至更优的性能。

Conclusion: CARE-LoRA通过压缩激活重建有效缓解了LoRA微调中的激活内存瓶颈，是一种高效且性能优异的PEFT方法。

Abstract: As the scale of large pre-trained models continues to grow, fine-tuning them under limited memory budgets has become increasingly challenging. Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), currently one of the most widely adopted parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, mitigates this challenge by optimizing only low-rank adaptation matrices, thereby greatly reducing the number of trainable parameters. With the parameter overhead substantially reduced, the activations retained for backpropagation have emerged as the primary remaining memory bottleneck during LoRA fine-tuning. To address this, we propose CARE-LoRA, a data-aware Compressed Activation REconstruction framework. By exploiting the inherent projection structure of LoRA, CARE-LoRA replaces the full input activation with the low-rank compressed activation naturally produced by the LoRA branch. It further computes a lightweight reconstruction matrix during the forward pass with negligible additional computation cost, which is used during backpropagation to reconstruct the gradient signal, thereby keeping LoRA matrices fully trainable. Extensive experiments across diverse models and downstream tasks demonstrate that, while substantially reducing the overall memory footprint, CARE-LoRA achieves competitive or even superior performance compared with standard LoRA and representative LoRA variants. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/fishandyu/CARE-LoRA .

</details>


### [107] [How Query Visibility Changes KV-Cache Compression Rankings: A Matched-Budget Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11942)
*Daming Luo,Christy Liang,Junyu Xuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了一个重要但被忽视的问题：KV缓存压缩方法大多在查询可见（query-aware）协议下评估，但实际部署中是查询不可见（query-agnostic）的。在匹配预算审计下，方法排名会因协议改变而翻转，且不同方法在两种协议间的性能下降程度与问题对其评分信号的可见性高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法评估普遍采用query-aware协议（压缩前查询已附在上下文后），但实际经济应用场景是复用——一次压缩文档，多次回答未来问题，此时压缩必须在查询不可见（query-agnostic）下进行。当前缺乏对两种协议差异的系统性审计。

Method: 对6种已发表压缩方法及3种简单基线，在3个7-9B模型上进行匹配预算审计（RULER-8192上144,300对评估，LongBench上40,800评估，5万次重采样配对bootstrap），严格控制模型、压缩率、实例、解码等变量，仅改变评分规则（query-aware vs query-agnostic）。

Result: (1) 查询可见性改变排名：query-agnostic协议下，5种共享注意力后端的审计方法中仅KeyDiff稳定优于最佳3选1简单基线（31/36单元格）；最广泛部署的SnapKV平均比"保留开头+近期窗口"基线差0.066。(2) 方法在两种协议间的性能下降程度（Delta）与问题对其评分信号的可见性顺序一致：SnapKV（问题在其64 token观察窗内）Delta=+0.198，KeyDiff（评分不含查询项）Delta=+0.011。

Conclusion: KV缓存压缩方法的评估协议必须与实际部署场景对齐。当前主流方法的query-aware评估高估了实际query-agnostic场景下的性能，方法排名在两种协议下发生翻转。研究呼吁在query-agnostic协议下重新评估压缩方法，并关注评分信号设计对部署鲁棒性的影响。

Abstract: KV-cache compression methods are predominantly evaluated with the query appended to the context before compression -- a query-aware protocol. Yet the economic case for a compressed KV cache is reuse: compress a document once, answer many future questions against it. In that deployment, compression must happen query-agnostic -- before any question is seen. We present a matched-budget audit of six published compression methods against three trivial baselines on three open 7-9B models (144,300 paired evaluations on RULER-8192; 40,800 on LongBench; 50,000-resample paired bootstrap throughout). Everything is held fixed -- model, compression ratio, instances, decoding -- except the scoring rule. Three findings. (1) Query visibility changes the rankings: under the agnostic protocol, of the five audited methods that share a common attention backend, only KeyDiff beats a best-of-3 trivial baseline consistently (31 of 36 cells), and the most widely deployed method, SnapKV, loses to "keep the start and the recent window" on average (-0.066). (2) The per-method drop between the two protocols is ordered consistently with how visible the question is to each method's scoring signal, legible in its source code: from Delta=+0.198 for SnapKV (the question sits inside its 64-token observation window) down to Delta=+0.011 for KeyDiff (its score contains no query term at all).

</details>


### [108] [BattVAE-GP: Generative Modeling of Long-Horizon Battery Degradation with Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11943)
*Raghvender Raghvender,Mahdi Abid,Ferran Brosa Planella,Charles Delacourt,Arnaud Demortière*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合物理-概率学习框架(BattVAE-GP)，结合变分自编码器与稀疏多任务高斯过程，对锂离子电池退化轨迹进行高效、带不确定性的代理建模，可准确预测未见充电倍率下的退化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于物理的电池退化模拟计算成本高昂，难以在长时间循环寿命下密集探索不同运行条件，亟需一种计算高效且能提供不确定性量化的替代方法。

Method: 1) 使用PyBaMM中DFN/P2D电化学模型生成循环级退化数据；2) 转化为容量对齐的电压及导数特征，用VAE编码到二维潜空间；3) 在潜空间中训练稀疏多任务高斯过程，以循环数和C倍率为输入，实现连续插值并给出后验不确定性；4) 通过VAE解码器将GP预测的潜状态还原为电压-容量演化，并通过蒙特卡洛传播获得SOH不确定性估计。

Result: 在协议级留出评估下，GP准确恢复未见C倍率轨迹，不确定性行为与训练数据支持一致；在未见中间C倍率下，生成的潜轨迹仍保持与相邻模拟协议之间的连贯位置关系。

Conclusion: BattVAE-GP为长时程退化建模提供了一种计算高效且具有不确定性意识的替代方案，为扩展电池健康预测至更丰富的运行条件及未来模拟-实验融合奠定了基础。

Abstract: Long-horizon physics-based simulations of battery degradation provide mechanistic insight but remain computationally expensive, limiting their use for dense exploration of operating conditions over extended cycle life. Here, we propose a hybrid physics-probabilistic learning framework for surrogate modeling of lithium-ion battery degradation trajectories at unseen charging rates. Cycle-resolved degradation data generated with a DFN/P2D electrochemical model in PyBaMM are first transformed into capacity-aligned voltage and derivative features and encoded using a Variational Autoencoder (VAE). The resulting two-dimensional latent space organizes degradation trajectories according to both cycle progression and charging protocol. A sparse multitask Gaussian process (GP) is then trained in this latent space using cycle number and C-rate as input variables, providing continuous interpolation of latent degradation dynamics together with posterior uncertainty estimates. Under protocol-level holdout evaluation, the latent-space GP accurately recovers unseen C-rate trajectories and exhibits uncertainty behavior consistent with the support of the training data. When queried at unseen interior C-rates, the model generates latent trajectories that remain coherently positioned between neighboring simulated protocols. Decoding the GP-predicted latent states through the frozen VAE decoder yields smooth voltage-capacity evolution, while Monte Carlo propagation of the GP latent posterior through an auxiliary latent to State of Health (SOH) predictor provides uncertainty-aware SOH estimates. The proposed BattVAE-GP framework therefore offers a computationally efficient and uncertainty-aware surrogate for long-horizon degradation modeling, providing a structured basis for extending battery health prediction toward richer operating conditions and future simulation-experiment fusion.

</details>


### [109] [Generalized Distribution-Free Semi-Supervised Learning with Risk Rewrite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11947)
*Yushi Hirose,Hiroo Irobe,Takafumi Kanamori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个广义半监督学习框架，通过线性组合组件风险构建无偏风险估计器，覆盖PNU学习并扩展至多分类，理论上证明可降低方差并提升泛化性能，实验验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SSL方法依赖分布假设，违背时性能下降；PNU学习虽无分布假设但限于二分类且方差最优性不明确。本文旨在构建一个广义、分布自由的框架，突破PNU局限并实现方差优化。

Method: 提出广义框架，通过线性组合组件风险构建无偏风险估计器，推导最小可达方差，建立方差降低与泛化性能提升的理论联系，并基于此设计两种实用SSL方法。

Result: 理论证明所提估计器在非对称损失下可达到比PNU更低的方差；泛化界建立方差降低与学习性能提升的直接联系；实验表明所提方法在二分类和多分类基准上匹配或超越现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提出框架统一并扩展了PNU学习，通过方差优化实现了更优的泛化性能，为分布自由的半监督学习提供了理论与实用价值。

Abstract: Typical semi-supervised learning (SSL) methods rely on distributional assumptions, and their performance degrades when these are violated. While PNU learning, a risk rewriting method, offers a distribution-free alternative, it is restricted to binary classification and its variance optimality remains unclear. In this paper, we propose a generalized framework that constructs unbiased risk estimators using linear combinations of component risks, subsuming PNU learning and extending to multiclass classification. We derive the minimum achievable variance, demonstrating our estimator can attain lower variance than PNU in asymmetric loss scenarios. Furthermore, we establish a generalization bound directly linking this variance reduction to improved learning performance. Based on these theoretical insights, we introduce two practical SSL methods that empirically match or outperform existing approaches on binary and multiclass benchmarks.

</details>


### [110] [Scale-Aware Attention for Scarce Neural Data: An RG-Flow Transformer on Sleep-EDF EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11950)
*Dibakar Sigdel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究了具有显式重正化群（RG）归纳偏置的RG-Flow Transformer与参数匹配的普通Transformer在真实、稀缺的EEG数据上的表现差异。在5类AASM睡眠分期任务中，两者在准确率上无统计显著差异（77.3% vs 77.0%），且稀缺数据下普通Transformer反而略优。关键区别在于可解释性：RG-Flow能够从EEG信号中恢复连续的谱指数β（R²=0.416），这是普通Transformer不具备的能力。


<details>
